## Changes currently active in private hardware

`git diff --stat -- chipyard`:
3 files, about 132 insertions / 19 deletions.

### 1. Checker direct delivery

File:
`chipyard/generators/rocket-chip/src/main/scala/rocket/Frontend.scala`

Implemented guarded **S2 classification → FQ direct delivery**, avoiding unconditional registered READY cycle. When CFI starts at current instruction and BJL head is actually ready and matches PC/RVC, reserve it **on direct FQ acceptance**, also avoiding WAIT_BJL cycle.

Details:
- Wires `checker_direct_emit`, `checker_direct_resp`, `checker_direct_next_pc`.
- `checker_direct_fire = direct_emit && fq.enq.ready`.
- `checker_direct_handoff` launches resolved successor on that same accepted delivery.
- s0 eligibility and npc mux include direct handoff.
- Direct response computes same prefix/masks/BTB target as registered path.
- Fault/replay never directly bypass; existing held path handles them.
- Missing/mismatched BJL → existing WAIT_BJL.
- FQ backpressure → capture existing response register, use existing fallback.
- Direct BJL reservation gated by actual direct FQ fire.
- Existing WAIT_BJL update narrowed to WAIT_BJL so direct reserve does not overwrite unrelated metadata.
- Partial RVC tail updates on direct FQ fire, tagged with old accepted fetch sequence.
- If no successor request fires, preserve resolved next PC in s1_pc.
- Redirect/clear retains original priority and kills ownership.
- Updated old assertions to allow direct classification delivery and reservation only under accepted direct conditions.
- Added ownership, prefix, matching BJL reservation, accepted-successor assertions.
- Existing kind13 emitted/handoff counters include direct deliveries.
- Existing kind16 fields **d11–d14** now contain cumulative local:
  1. direct deliveries
  2. direct BJL reservations
  3. direct successor launches
  4. direct candidate blocked by FQ
  Earlier d0–d10 unchanged.
- No new checker observer identity.

Cycle rationale: original hit accepted S0 → S1 → S2 classify → READY delivery, branch extra WAIT_BJL. New common path delivers at S2, launches next S0 then. Still only one response ownership, same registered fallback.

### 2. L2 SourceC waitAck overlap

Files:
- `chipyard/generators/sifive-cache/design/craft/inclusivecache/src/L2Preservation.scala`
- same directory `Scheduler.scala`

Applied functional portion of archived
`slowdown/analysis_archive/experimental_patches/sourcec_experiment.patch`
(excluded its old observation hunk, which depends on absent post-Opt4 observer helpers).

- L2Preservation exports `waitingAck := state === waitAck`.
- Assert no bank read/A valid in pure waitAck.
- Scheduler `sourceCReserved = lock.valid && !waitingAck`.
- Substitute narrower reservation in SourceC schedule gate and shared SourceC bank read mux/ready.
- **Target-set lock remains through matching terminal ACK**, all physical overwrite guards preserved.
- SourceC accepted request asserts no port reservation and no target-set conflict.
- Before new capture original drain of SourceC remains, preventing overlap with next checkpoint capture.
- Preserves safe watermark, source/transaction/size ACK validation, error states.

Added **SC48**, one mandatory forced STOP sample using existing observationEnable/reset:

## Cache invariants and promising next work

Audited:
`boom/lsu/dcache.scala`, `mshrs.scala`, `speculative-version-buffer.scala`, `phase1.scala`; L2 Scheduler/L2Preservation/SourceC/SourceD/MSHR.

- L1 demand loads use ordinary current tag/data; SVB never selected for ordinary load.
- Key = writer, generation, contentSeq; epoch guards incarnation.
- Unsafe predecessor copied immutably to SPEC before younger store overwrites.
- SVB entry safe only current epoch, seq≤watermark/untracked/zero.
- Coalescing only superseded safe SPEC, same line/writer, sequence/generation.
- WB owner retained through matching ACK, not just last C.
- Younger same-line release/probe blocked through retained predecessor ACK.
- Failed/cancelled result never advances prefix; accepted contiguous PASS prefix advances safe watermark.
- Watermark does not rewrite tags.
- Existing `svbFlush := io.traffic_reset || io.lsu.exception` discards SPEC entries and increments epoch on exception. Failure invalidation instead discards only seq>watermark. **Review this existing recovery boundary carefully before broadening safe snapshot dependence**; do not assume full recovery proven.

Two prepared NOT-active DCache patches:

1. `safe_predecessor_svb.patch`
   Removes just `!L1Preservation.safe(s2_overwrittenKey, safePacketWatermark)` from `svbEligibleCandidate` (~line1790). Idea: safe predecessor lacking lower receipt currently takes destructive legacy WB+invalidation, although existing immutable SVB capture/promotion could preserve it asynchronously. Retains M_XWR/LSU/cacheable/nonzero/incomingSeq>oldSeq/validKey/capacity/descriptor/lease checks. Needs safety review, especially epoch flush/recovery, and instrument eligibility reasons. Old “unsupported4774” includes resource guards, not proof 4774 safe predecessors.

2. `store_completion_overlap.patch`
   - `s2_write_busy = s3_valid` rather than `s3_valid || completionQ.count=/=0` (~1264), relying on existing per-slot `completionPending` owner gate to block same slot while unrelated stores proceed.
   - completionArb.in(0).valid includes `s3_effect_fire` (~3113), allowing terminal enqueue on actual physical store effect rather than waiting extra s3_applied register cycle.
   Physical effect accepted before queue completion next cycle; data/result already captured in S2. Cancellation/failedSC/duplicate paths retain old behavior. Need directed validation and counters. More aggressive simultaneous S3 retire/new grant would require careful register assignment priority and same-line generation forwarding, **not implemented**.

Other idea considered, not implemented:
- L2 checkpoint shared read port can theoretically arbitrate on actual `bs_adr.valid`, permitting normal SourceC during read delay/send as well as waitAck. SourceC tracks accepted addresses via S2/S3; checkpoint read→delay1→delay2 likewise. Target-set locks remain. But measure current waitAck result first.
- Avoid speculative removal of L2 old-safe checkpoint just because incoming safe image is newer: full recovery source assumptions not proven.
- Wider checker fetch could be future direction, but Rocket `fetchWidth=2` for compressed and `decodeWidth=fetchWidth/2`; would need decoupling scalar decode width and ICache fetchBytes. Not implemented.

## 11. 助手 · 2026-11-03T01:22:09.369Z

## 12. 助手 · 2026-11-03T01:24:01.635Z

The “safe predecessor” shortcut has a recovery concern: the existing exception flush can discard an SVB snapshot even after its sequence becomes safe. Routing more safe recovery copies through SVB would therefore need a separate retention fix, so I’m leaving that candidate inactive. Completion overlap has a narrower proof: the effect and return value are already captured before enqueue, and existing guards prevent the affected slot from being overwritten or reassigned before completion.

## 13. 助手 · 2026-11-03T01:26:26.749Z

RTL generation caught a real issue before simulation: the direct frontend path forms a combinational loop through Rocket’s redirect logic and the fetch queue’s flow-through output. I’m separating response validity from accepted delivery. Redirects will still cancel reservations, handoffs, and state updates; validity will follow the same convention as the existing buffered path.

## 14. 助手 · 2026-11-03T01:28:59.415Z

In the saved baseline prefix, each checker spends about two local cycles fetching and one READY cycle per delivery; ICache misses are uncommon in that evidence. That supports testing the direct path, though those checker cycles are not equivalent to BOOM runtime savings. The corrected version is now in RTL generation, and I’ve added diagnostic checks that reject inconsistent overlap counts.

## 15. 助手 · 2026-11-03T01:32:56.357Z

The completion-overlap model passes its bounded backpressure checks, including full queues, same-slot reuse, failed SC, duplicate tokens, and cancellation. These are CPU contract tests, not RTL validation; the candidate remains separate. I’m also preparing a frontend follow-up that delivers a straight-line prefix and its first logged branch together when the matching branch entry is available.

## 16. 助手 · 2026-11-03T01:36:47.071Z

The LSU audit found why removing the DCache queue gate alone would have limited value: it also blocks every younger store until older stores report terminal completion. I’ve prepared a combined candidate that delivers an already accepted physical effect through an empty completion queue and lets the next store enter S0 on that completion edge. Unresolved predecessors will continue to block later stores.

## 17. 助手 · 2026-11-03T01:39:01.214Z

The corrected frontend passed FIRRTL’s combinational-loop check. The build has moved on to Verilog generation. The experiment remains at four checkers with the same clocks and cache capacities; the performance candidates are direct response delivery and SourceC overlap during checkpoint ACK wait.

## Active hardware changes — only three files

### Checker direct delivery

`chipyard/generators/rocket-chip/src/main/scala/rocket/Frontend.scala`

Owned S2 response classifies and **delivers directly to FQ**, avoiding READY cycle. CFI at start can also reserve an available matching BJL head on actual direct acceptance, avoiding WAIT_BJL.

Preserves:
- Fault/replay registered fallback
- Missing/mismatched BJL → WAIT_BJL
- FQ backpressure → held register fallback
- Reservation only on non-killed accepted direct delivery
- Partial RVC tail uses accepted fetch’s old sequence
- Redirect/clear priority and request ownership
- Resolved successor launch same delivery edge, or held next PC if ICache refuses

New assertions check direct ownership, prefixes, BJL reservation, successor ownership.
Kind13 counts include direct delivery/handoff.
Kind16 **d11–d14** cumulative local counters:
1. direct deliveries
2. direct BJL reservations
3. direct successor launches
4. FQ-blocked direct candidates

**Important fix made in this resumed turn:** initial implementation failed FIRRTL with four combinational loops:
FQ flow-through valid → Rocket redirect → classification valid → direct enqueue valid.
Current implementation separates:
- `checker_classify_candidate`: owned S2 candidate, independent of `io.cpu.req.valid`
- `checker_classify_fire`: candidate && !redirect
- `checker_direct_emit`: candidate, **not redirect-gated**
- `checker_direct_fire`: emit && FQ ready && !redirect

FQ VALID follows existing READY-path convention; redirect kills IBuf and resets FQ, while reservations/state/handoffs remain redirect-gated. Current build passed comb-loop checks.

### L2 SourceC waitAck overlap

- `chipyard/generators/sifive-cache/design/craft/inclusivecache/src/L2Preservation.scala`
- same dir `Scheduler.scala`

`waitingAck` output; `sourceCReserved = lock.valid && !waitingAck`.
Allows unrelated SourceC requests/shared bank reads in pure waitAck, retaining target-set lock through matching terminal ACK, all overwrite/publication guards, and drain before next capture.

Mandatory **SC48** STOP counters:
- d0 schema1
- d1 sample cycles
- d2 narrowed reserved cycles
- d3 reserved & pending voluntary release
- d4 accepted voluntary releases during waitAck
- d5 pending release & no acceptance
- d6 waitAck cycles
- d7 waitAck & pending release
- d8 target-lock cycles (old reservation shadow on new schedule)
- d9 target-lock & pending release
- d10–15 zero

Counts are overlapping activity, **not causal savings**.

**No ROI bug here:** `SafeChainObservation.context()._2` is a **sticky capture flag**, set at traffic start and held through tail, not software ROI/traffic enable. SC48 STOP can emit correctly. Do not “fix” it based on assuming ROI falls. Conversely, future frontend final snapshots must **not** trigger on falling `opt2bRoi`, which never falls until reset. Use `GH_L2_STATS_ENABLE_BORE` falling edge and clearly label traffic-window counts.

## Prepared candidates — NOT active yet

All in `candidates/`.

### 1. `terminal_bypass.patch` — promising primary cache candidate

Supersedes simpler `store_completion_overlap.patch`.

Source audit found:
- DCache globally blocks writes while **any** completion is queued.
- More importantly LSU `can_fire_store_commit` blocks all younger stores while **any older functional_inflight** remains, through terminal delivery, even after physical data effect.
- Removing DCache queue count alone therefore offers little.

Combined patch:
1. Native completionQ `flow=true`.
2. `s2_write_busy = s3_valid` (per-slot completion guards stay).
3. Allow completionArb.in0 valid on `s3_effect_fire`, rather than waiting extra `s3_applied` cycle. S3 return/data already captured in S2.
4. Dequeue owner assertion allows identical **same-edge enqueue payload**, with matching live slot epoch, when empty queue bypasses; queued results still require completionPending.
5. LSU adds `storeTerminalForward` / index wires. Older-inflight predicate excludes only identity-checked **terminal actually accepted this edge**.
6. Disable forwarding if `olderStoreNackForcesReplay`.
7. Existing terminal handler remains sole STQ release. Next younger request enters S0 only after/with older actual physical effect, never passes unresolved predecessor.

Correctness notes:
- Full Q holds S3; applied prevents duplicate write.
- Failed SC/duplicate/cancel retain original registered behavior.
- Cancellation never forwards because store_done.valid is false for cancel.
- Slot install/refill/probe/metadata drains guard completionPending/S3.
- MSHR reservation alone does not change resident slotEpoch; physical install does, guarded.
- **Do not additionally implement simultaneous S3 retire/new S2 grant:** same-slot image-generation forwarding and register assignment priorities need separate proof.
- Could recover up to multiple serial latency cycles per store; actual critical gain unknown. About61k semantic writes baseline; not a measured stall attribution.

Audit:
`CANDIDATE_AUDIT.md`

### 2. `prefix_cfi_fusion.patch`

If first normal CFI occurs after a straight-line prefix and matching BJL head is ready:
- Deliver prefix **and CFI together**, mark branch’s end in existing bjlEndMask.
- Reserve on actual direct accepted delivery.
- Avoid refetching same word at CFI PC.
- If log unavailable/mismatched, retain original prefix-only progress/fallback.
- Original held classifier unchanged.

IBuf already carries end-mask ownership through prior instructions. Rollback returns speculative BJL reservations to committed pointer.

### 3. `checker_fetch8.patch`

Three files:
- RocketCore.scala: `fetchWords:Int=1`, fetchWidth scales with words, scalar decodeWidth fixed1; require1or2.
- subsystem/Configs.scala: only WithNGCCheckers uses fetchWords2 and ICache fetchBytes8.
- IBuf.scala: system instruction recognition checks head low32/16 bits rather than entire fetched word; suppress C.EBREAK as **16-bit C.NOP**, not 32-bit nop, preserving consumed length.

Keeps checker count, scalar retire/decode, cache capacity/set/ways/line sizes and clocks. Changes instruction supply width/SRAM organization only. Needs actual IBuf tests before full benchmark.

### 4. `safe_predecessor_svb.patch` — WITHHELD

One-line removal of unsafe-only eligibility would route safe predecessors without lower receipt through SVB instead of destructive legacy writeback/invalidation.

**Do not apply as written.**
Existing `svbFlush := traffic_reset || lsu.exception` discards all SPEC entries, even safe ones, while WB_WAIT survives. New dependence on safe SPEC could lose recovery data before promotion on exception. Needs explicit retention across epoch boundaries. Failure-only invalidate does keep safe SPEC, but is not the only boundary.
This is not proof baseline full recovery is correct.

## Key invariants and recovered bottlenecks

- Demand loads use ordinary L1 tags/data, never SVB.
- Key writer/generation/contentSeq; epoch guards incarnation.
- Immutable predecessor captured before younger write.
- Entry safe by watermark, selected/coalesced sequence+generation.
- WB ownership retained until matching ACK; younger same-line release/probe blocked through WB_WAIT.
- Only contiguous accepted PASS prefix advances watermark. FAIL/CANCELLED do not.
- Watermark does not rewrite tags.

- Baseline excess706,188 cycles.
- Scheduler unavailable267,087 in complete optional prefix, not exact recoverable gain.
- Legacy pending52,702 prefix cycles,47,827 already safe.
- SVB32 fixed-trace bound5254 cycles; low priority.
- Promotion safe→terminal union24569 exposure, not gain.
- L1 MSHR busy315637 union; full0.
- Unsupported fallback4774 **events**, not cycles or all safe predecessors.
- L164KiB result1,648,923 reference-only, strict pairing not closed.
- Prior “frontend last resort / no runs” historical instructions superseded by current explicit autonomous request.

## Active B hardware changes

Six unstaged files beyond A:
- `boom/.../lsu/dcache.scala`
- `boom/.../lsu/lsu.scala`
- `rocket/.../Frontend.scala`
- `rocket/.../IBuf.scala`
- `rocket/.../RocketCore.scala`
- `rocket/.../subsystem/Configs.scala`

Changes:
1. **Terminal bypass**
   - DCache completionQ `flow=true`
   - Remove global queued-completion write blocker; per-slot pending guards remain
   - Terminal enqueue permitted on actual `s3_effect_fire`
   - Empty flow-through dequeue ownership allows identical same-edge accepted payload with live epoch
   - LSU older functional-inflight barrier excludes only identity-matched terminal actually accepted this edge
   - Older replay/nack disables forwarding
   - Existing handler remains sole STQ release
   - FullQ holds appliedS3 without duplicate effect
   - FailedSC/duplicate/cancel registered handling retained
   - Do not add simultaneous S3 turnover/new S2 grant without separate proof.

   Conditional source timing example: S0n→S2grantn+2→effectn+3.
   Old terminalenqueue n+4, deliver n+5, nextstoreS0 n+6.
   B canenqueue/deliver and nextstoreS0 n+3 under free response/emptyQ.
   This is not measured savings.

2. **Prefix+CFI fusion**
   - If firstCFI follows prefix and matching BJL head ready, direct-deliver prefix+CFI together using existing end mask
   - Reserve only on actual acceptance
   - Otherwise retain original prefix-only/held fallback

3. **Checker fetch8**
   - `RocketCoreParams(fetchWords=1default)`, supports1or2
   - fetchWidth scales; decode/retire remain1
   - OnlyWithNGCCheckers usesfetchWords2 /ICachefetchBytes8
   - No cache geometry/clock/count changes
   - IBuf syscall detection checks only head32/16bits
   - C.EBREAK suppressed to **C.NOP16bit**, preserves consumed length
   - ECALL/EBREAK→NOP32bit

4. **Diagnostics**
   - Four mandatory **SC49**, harts1–4: full hardware traffic-window frontend counters
   - One **SC50** DCache/LSU final snapshot
   - Expected observer identities **127** /128limit
   - Do not blindly add mandatory identities.
   - `SafeChainObservation.context()._2` is sticky capture flag, **not falling ROI**.
   - SC49 STOP uses falling `GH_L2_STATS_ENABLE_BORE`
   - SC50 STOP uses fallingtrafficCounting, includesSTOPedge.
   - Counters only logger inputs, no functional feedback.

d0schema1,d1samplelocalcycles,d2live,d3empty,d4fetching,d5retiredclassify0,d6waitBJL,d7ready,d8deliveries,d9handoff,d10I$requests,d11heldready,d12faultdeliveries,d13I$acquires,d14TLBmiss,d15redirect.

### Mechanisms and attribution limits

A:
- Direct owned/classified S2 checker response→FQ and successor launch on acceptance.
- BJL matching reservation only actual accepted delivery; fallback/backpressure/redirect intact.
- SourceC allowed during pure checkpoint waitAck, while target set lock/ACK owner retained.
Combined gain281018cycles; no isolated component attribution. SourceC overlap exercised3times A,1 B,3 C, so don't assign large savings to it.

B:
- terminal enqueue on accepted physical effect edge.
- completionQ flow=true; remove global queued-completion admission blocker, retain per-slot guards.
- LSU successor S0 only bypasses exact older terminal accepted same edge; ordinary STQ terminal handler sole release, nack priority.
- fetch8,prefix+firstCFI fusion if matching readyBJL.
- IBuf head-only syscall detection,C.EBREAK→16bitC.NOP preserves length.
Combined gain252071cycles; no isolated component ROI.

C:
- Safe old T resident publishes full ReleaseData **TtoT**.
- Same freeze/drain/source/epoch/key/exact ACK.
- On matching receipt keep tags/data/key/epoch/effect identity, setlowerPreserved,releasefreeze.
- Branch fallback still invalidates; probe mismatch/revoke cannot resurrect line.
- Next physical write revokes receipt; L2 historical protection unchanged.
Gain46722cycles vsB measured as whole candidate. Avoids clear/refill but phase counters not additive causal savings.

Withheld safeSPEC SVB candidate: exceptions flush SPEC including safeentries; epoch-qualifiedselection. Do not apply.

## Frozen correction FAILED — real correctness bug

Run:
`correction_test4_20261103_064146_55b93744`
original sealed C binary.
- exit1,43.894805835559964sec to failure.
- no ROI completion, no ratio.
- assert in generated
`.../build/overlap_retained_copy_20261103_040650_0a6ef6bc/generated/gen-collateral/BoomNonBlockingDCache.sv:118954`
`assert__assert_30` maps source `dcache.scala:1883`:
`svbCapturePending && svbCaptureIssued` on snapshot acceptance.
- printed `[30000]` is rounded `$time`; failure event core cycle **30045/0x755d**.
- Detailed evidence and causal chain in
`slowdown/blackscholes_validation/CORRECTNESS_FINDING.md`.
- Failed stdout/capture/ring/rawevents gzip preserved under evidence.

Event chain:
- cycle30018/7542 WB accepts snapshot read line8002bec0.
- 30025/7549 first capture accepted.
- 30029/754d matching semantic write consumes lease.
- 30038/7556 WB accepts second read line8002be80,keyseq1/gen851.
- 30040/7558 SC35 flush-discard event.
- 30045/755d late WB snapshot allocated in new epoch; ownerassert.
Source:
`svbFlush := io.traffic_reset || io.lsu.exception`.
Old flush unconditionally cleared pending/issued, but WB has no cancel input and completes already accepted row read. SVB only blocks capture on flush edge, then accepts old snapshot with new epoch.
Cause strongly supported/proven source+SC35; exact trap cause not captured (normal architectural interrupts/exceptions, no fault injection).

### Correctness repair currently building

Only `dcache.scala` modified on validation branch:
- Add `svbCaptureCancelled`.
- `svbCaptureDiscard = cancelled || svbFlush`.
- `wb.snapshot_ready = discard || svb.capture.ready`.
- `svb.capture.valid = wb.snapshot_valid && !discard`.
- Block new WB capture issue on svbFlush.
- Consume snapshot through one predicate, retain pending/issued and immutable key/line/slot epoch until response.
- Flush of issued request sets cancelled and retains freeze/descriptor.
- Unissued request cancels immediately.
- Late/same-edge-flush response drains without SVB allocation or lease.
- Keep all owner/identity assertions; add live-consume requires capture and cancelled consume forbids allocation.
- Existing normal SVB flush policy unchanged (known durable recovery gap not solved).
- Diagnostic `SVB_CAPTURE_DISCARD ...` retained by WorkloadRouter subclass in validation runner; counted `cancelled_snapshots_drained`.

    ```
      svbCaptureResidentMatch && same set/way/tag/seq/slot epoch...
    s3_preservationAllowed = L1Preservation.allow(...) || s3_svbOwnsPredecessor
    ```
  - ~3126 asserts preservation for live not-applied, nonduplicate, uncancelled S3 write.
  - ~3368 dataWriteArb effect clears lease on matching semantic write.
  - ~3420 unconditional svbFlush clears lease and SPEC snapshots.
  - Existing `s3_pre_effect_kill` only kills exception request when uses_ldq; a **committed normal store survives exception**, so clearing its predecessor lease can invalidate an already granted S3 store.
- **Likely cause, not yet fully proven**: epoch flush revokes SVB bypass ownership between S2 grant and S3 effect (possibly same cycle S2 grants+flush, or held S3 grant).
- No RTL patch yet. Need reconstruct exact S2→S3 ordering using diagnostic now running.

### Running failure diagnostic (last tool action)

Session **23940**:
private Python capture of exact frozen Streamcluster failing ELF/model, added `+verbose`, max240000, no safe/cache observer flags, stdout+stderr full gzip.
Directory:
`slowdown/parsec_evaluation_20261103/build/streamcluster_preservation_diagnostic/`
- command.json exists
- raw.log.gz growing
- on finish tail.log (last1200 lines), result.json
- Expected repeat assertion, **excluded performance**.
- Original full verbose prints STAGE1_TX S1/S2/ISSUE/EFFECT/KILL and committed BOOM PCs. Read tail to prove grant/flush timeline.
- Does not use run_pilot seal checks; model and copied failing ELF unchanged even if source later edited.

### Proposed correctness fix direction (NOT IMPLEMENTED)

Do not remove assertion or cancel/drop an already committed store.

Consider:
1. Block new S2 write grants on a requested SVB epoch flush.
2. Defer the actual SVB flush/epoch change while an existing live, unapplied, noncancelled S3 grant **depends on its SVB lease**, allowing that already owned store to reach its physical effect/terminal.
3. Latch a pending flush request so a one-cycle exception is not lost. Apply flush once dependent S3 drains; advance epoch on actual flush, not original pulse.
4. Preserve the prior late-capture cancellation repair (pending snapshot with issued reader remains until response discarded). When capture pending and no live lease, flush should remain immediate.
5. No new captures/grants should cross pending epoch boundary.
6. Need avoid combinational loop; define early Wire for dependent S3 drain, assign after s3_svbOwnsPredecessor and pre_effect_kill are defined. Dependency on registered S3/image state, not fire.
7. May need latch reset-vs-exception reason if delaying traffic_reset; routine traffic reset occurs outside live lease, but define semantics explicitly.
8. Minimal alternative just gate S2 grant on flush could fix observed same-edge case but leaves held-S3/array-backpressure race; test both timing boundaries.
9. Existing SVB component tests do **not** cover DCache S2/S3 lease; add meaningful directed controller timing test plus full-system failing workload regression.
10. Flush discarding SPEC historical states is a separately documented durable-recovery gap; this correctness repair should not claim to implement architectural rollback.

If fixed:
- New explicit hardware revision/tag (do not silently move old canonical tag).
- Rebuild corrected full system with established `slowdown/optimization_6_overlap/scripts/run_experiment.py --build-only --label ...`; inspect flow first.
- Reuse caches/toolchain, no FPGA changes.
- Must rerun BFS and Blackscholes before accepting revised hardware, plus failed Streamcluster.
- Update all source/model seals and runner’s canonical/source hash/model references; current runner hardcodes old build and source seal and will correctly refuse changed source.
- New native mode may need new-model regression to confirm cycles; detection unchanged and its identical-ELF runs can be reused.
- Performance evaluation cannot treat old failed design as final for whole suite.

## 210. 助手 · 2026-11-04T00:37:44.992Z

## 当前状态

- 用户要求测完 PARSEC 的不同 benchmark，并明确反对只围绕 Blackscholes 重复跑。此前 runner 串行等待 Blackscholes 的问题已纠正：Blackscholes project64、Streamcluster project64、Canneal project400 三组 Native/Check/Correction 已分别启动。
- 最近观测时三组仍在运行，事件流周期约为 Blackscholes 1.48M、Streamcluster 0.40M、Canneal 0.21M；每次最多 10M cycles。尚无主测量 triplet 最终结果。
- 后台收尾器 `finalize_running_parsec_batches.py`（PID `973494`）等待这三批结束，随后检查等价性与 correctness，重跑有效 workload 的 correction，并执行汇总。批次 IDs：`measurements_124710_1426a31f`、`measurements_131425_0ee6393e`、`measurements_131916_46c7c03f`。
- 旧的 `overnight_parsec_remaining.py`（PID `796311`）已停止；不要再启动它。可从 `slowdown/parsec_evaluation_20261103/logs/parsec_main_run.latest` 查看收尾报告。

## 已冻结硬件

- Checkout：`/data1/gzh/EC-opt4-perf`
- 分支：`opt4/final-eval-20261103`，最近代码提交 `a0833a772`
- Canonical hardware commit：`a6e63c75947739dfbb411b32bfe0eb5ed55c5bb1`
- Tag：`opt4-final-corrected-r2-20261103`
- BFS、Blackscholes、Streamcluster、Canneal 和 emitted epoch controller acceptance 均通过。BFS ROI 为 1,128,052 cycles。
- 修复后 simdev pilot triplet 已通过，FAIL/CANCELLED 均为 0。接受记录、复现清单、输入计划和 runner 均已提交。

## 用户偏好与约束

- 用中文，少查询、少中间汇报，节省 token；但不要隐瞒失败或未完成项。
- 不要继续性能 RTL 优化，除非发现 correctness bug。
- 不要修改 `/data1/gzh/EC`：那里有并行 FPGA 工作；`/data1/gzh/EC/check/source` 是只读历史源码。所有工作在 `/data1/gzh/EC-opt4-perf`。
- 不使用子代理，不执行历史 FireSim 脚本。保留失败 run 和原始日志。

## 尚未完成的重点

用户要的是 PARSEC **不同 benchmark**。目前 13 项清单里只有 Blackscholes、Streamcluster、Canneal 有现成 HTIF bare-metal 适配；其余 10 项还没有测量。支持清单在 `slowdown/parsec_evaluation_20261103/support_inventory.json` 和 `SUPPORT_MATRIX.md`。已安装的一些 RISC-V ELF 是 GNU/Linux 用户程序；当前仓库搜索未找到现成 `vmlinux`/rootfs，但 `qemu-riscv64` 和 Chipyard 环境里的 `qemu-system-riscv64` 可用。不能把 QEMU 主机运行结果当成硬件 ROI cycles。

后续应先确认三组已启动测量及收尾报告，然后实质推进其余 10 项：逐项检查官方源码、输入、依赖和 serial kernel 可裁剪性，尝试构建/适配可运行的 bare-metal ROI；若必须依赖 Linux，则记录具体构建或启动阻碍，并明确标为不可测，不能仅凭“当前不支持”结束。按 workload 分批，避免九个以上 Verilator 实例长期争抢资源。扩充支持矩阵，分别记录 builds、runs、ROI、Native/Check/Correction 和失败原因。

## 已确认的瓶颈证据

### 不应先盲改大核 cache 或水位

旧流量窗口：
- Streamcluster L1 physical miss=233、MSHR full cycles=0；
  L2 checkpoint ACK wait=36,934；
  SVB max16、same-line depth3。
- Canneal L1 miss=8,707、MSHR full=225；
  L2 checkpoint ACK wait=19,894；
  SVB max16、depth4。
- 这些窗口不同于 application ROI；特别 Canneal 包含约85万 setup cycles，不能相加解释应用慢化。
- cache shadow geometry 验证失败，**禁止使用其预测做容量结论**。
- Streamcluster 旧 optional checker counter 保存仅287/1213 包，原活动率12.84%只是下界。不能拿它排除 checker。
- 完整 Canneal checker counters 可用。

### Canneal 的安全水位等待主要在等 checker

从完整 Canneal SC1/SC8、接受结果/形成结果事件直接重建 ROI 等待（已落盘 `baseline/canneal_legacy_waits.json`）：

- `RESULT_NOT_AVAILABLE` 118,043 cycles：
  - checker execution/tail 尚未完成：**117,975**
  - 已形成结果但 transport/cleanup 未结束：68
- watermark register propagation：**17 cycles**
- POST_SAFE_WAIT：36,778
- MULTI_CAUSE：3,148

因此主要不是水位寄存器传播；不要放宽水位、ACK 或丢版本。

### Checker 取指压力

Canneal 完整 ROI 包：
- instruction_starvation = **268,029 checker-local cycles**；
- issue_available411,782；
- dependency108,205；
- management104,131；
- result queue backpressure=0。
- 四 checker 的保存 ROI frontend 窗口合计 ICache acquisitions **11,794**；
  L2 更宽窗口 physical misses1292，说明大量 I$ refill 是反复取代码/L2 hit。
- `baseline/canneal_frontend.json` 含精确每 hart 窗口及未覆盖边界。
- 这些 local cycles 不可当作 BOOM 可直接省下周期。

This result is substantially better than the initial 2.20% small-input improvement. Treat Candidate 1 as an important optimization milestone, not a marginal experiment.

### 1. Finish Candidate 2

Allow its current simulation to complete. Collect the actual Canneal main-input result and correctness evidence.

Do not relaunch any already completed tests.

### 2. Compare Candidate 1 and Candidate 2

Compare both candidates against the exact frozen baseline using matching benchmark inputs and configurations.

Report exact ROI cycles, Correction/Check ratios, and improvements for:

- Canneal
- Streamcluster
- Blackscholes
- GAPBS BFS

Select the best correctness-preserving candidate based on cross-workload performance, with special emphasis on Canneal and Streamcluster.

### 3. Investigate the large input-dependent gain

Candidate 1 improved Canneal by only 2.20% on the small input but 18.01% on the main input.

Determine the hardware reason for this difference.

Investigate whether the gain is associated with cache version occupancy, replacement conflicts, protected ways, additional refills, SourceC/ACK blocking, or checker backpressure.

Use existing profiling data first. Do not invent a mechanism explanation without supporting counters.

### 4. Evaluate combining the two candidates

If Candidate 1 and Candidate 2 modify compatible mechanisms, evaluate a combined implementation in a separate experimental branch.

Do not assume their performance benefits are additive.

Run targeted correctness checks before performance simulation.

Keep the combined implementation only if it improves the overall result without significant regressions.

### 5. Validate actual recovery correctness

This is mandatory before accepting the new hardware as a correct recovery implementation.

The previous Blackscholes experiments did not adequately exercise persistent snapshots.

Perform targeted tests that create and retain recovery-required versions, inject a checker-detectable fault, trigger FAIL and rollback, and verify restoration of historical data.

Include snapshot ownership, flush, ACK, safe-watermark boundaries, and cache-version reclamation.

Do not regard no-fault benchmark execution as sufficient evidence of recovery correctness.

### 6. Preserve the best validated version

Create an immutable tag and reproducible backup of the best implementation after correctness validation.

Do not replace the original three-benchmark baseline.

Do not modify FPGA or detection-only work.

Give particular attention to Streamcluster, which may remain above 1.4×.

Prioritize measured dominant bottlenecks rather than small speculative RTL changes.

Avoid increasing checker count.

- Candidate 1 and Candidate 2 exact results.
- Combined-candidate result if applicable.
- Best hardware commit and backup.
- Exact ROI cycles and slowdown for all four workloads.
- Explanation of Candidate 1's large Canneal improvement.

Reuse existing software by **copying or read-only symlinking individual build workload directories** from C1/C2 into new:
`slowdown/parsec_evaluation_20261103/build/{streamcluster_simdev,streamcluster_project64,canneal_project400,blackscholes_project64}`.
These dirs absent new worktree. `run_pilot` only reads build.manifest/ELFs and copies to fresh run; it will not build software.

compiles actual emitted SVB and C++ driver; existing scenarios unsafe16×1024cycles, invalidation, promotion backpressure1024, WB flush/epoch/ACK1024, same-line512, wrong-key/entry ACK assertions.
  Must expand for new safeAtCapture cases before performance.

- `test_emitted_rescue_gate.py <emitted DCache.sv>` Boolean cone20k vectors.
- `test_emitted_rescue_pulses.py`, `test_epoch_private.py` actual generated cone.

## Recovery evidence and limitation (critical)

Prior independent validation in:
`/data1/gzh/EC-opt4-recovery-validation/slowdown/recovery_perf_20261104/MECHANISM_AND_RECOVERY.md`
and `validation/`.

C1 private harness links exact C1 model read-only, flipped captured checker hart1 x31 bit:
- tick107179 x31 0x823→0x822, live16 SPEC snapshots retained>=1024, safe watermark0.
- tick107241 real comparator error.
- tick107304 real accepted FAIL sticky seq1; all16unsafe SPEC entries seq1/2 invalidated, flush0.
- subsequent PRESERVATION_WAIT with seq1/safe0.
- probe ends tick111400, no restored-state proof.

C2 stricter probe:
`.../build/real_checker_fault_20261104_083111`
required minwatermark1+liveWB_WAIT+persistent unsafe. It has finished (process absent); **result has not been read yet this turn**. Could be notexercised; do not assume injected. Check exit.json/logs if useful; no rerun.

Source:
- SVB no recovery read/restore interface; ordinary loads don’t select entries.
- `io.recoveryFromSvb := io.acked` counts promotionACK, not actual rollback.
- real failure blocks contiguous safe watermark/invalidate unsafe versions/L2 barrier.
- BOOM `l2BarrierErrorAny` synchronized but no downstream consumer found.
- no checkerFAIL→architectural PC/register/memory restore/replay link established.
- Therefore candidate A can be performance-provisional; cannot final recovery-accept. User explicitly expects repair if recovery fails, but don’t fabricate a restoration result by treating component payload or no-fault output as actual rollback.

## Next steps

1. Run/test/strengthen experiment cache; index existing exact artifacts, record no-repeat decisions.
2. Audit safeAtCapture redesign carefully, especially irreversible ownership through flush/coalescing/cross-epoch ordering. Add explicit invariants/meaningful directed emitted-RTL tests.
3. Commit isolated code and report. Start **one unique candidate A build** after cache confirms no equivalent compiled artifact. Don’t alter original active C2 tree.
4. While compilation runs, add tests/cache runner, analyze checker dependency or unsafe fallback reason for next major candidate if A insufficient; don’t build speculative variants without evidence.
5. After emitted tests pass, run only new short Streamcluster simdev pilot (no previous matching A revision result). Preserve failure logs. Compare to cached C1 pilot but don’t assume main prediction.
6. If promising main-input safe-copy mechanism, run A Streamproject64 then Canneal gate. Final Black/BFS only shortlist.
7. Recheck original Black naturally; collect final if done, compute exact C2 baseline/geomeans; never rerun.
8. Keep provisional performance separate from recovery acceptance; true recovery oracle mandatory before final accepted hardware.
9. Track saved baseline runs, actual new runs, failures/rejections, area/timing unmeasured. Preserve best validated/provisional evidence without replacing any baseline.
